{"cmd": "python3 - <<'PY'\nimport os, json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nroots = [Path('/mnt/private/models'), Path([REDACTED])]\nexts = {'.safetensors', '.bin', '.ckpt', '.pt', '.onnx', '.gguf'}\njson_ext = {'.json'}\n\nsummary = {}\nall_errors = []\n\n\ndef human(n):\n    units = ['B','KB','MB','GB','TB']\n    i = 0\n    n = float(n)\n    while n >= 1024 and i < len(units)-1:\n        n /= 1024\n        i += 1\n    return f\"{n:.2f}{units[i]}\"\n\n\ndef check_safetensors_header(path: Path):\n    with path.open('rb') as f:\n        first8 = f.read(8)\n        if len(first8) < 8:\n            return False, 'file too small (<8 bytes)'\n        header_len = int.from_bytes(first8, 'little', signed=False)\n        file_size = path.stat().st_size\n        if header_len <= 0:\n            return False, f'invalid header length {header_len}'\n        if 8 + header_len > file_size:\n            return False, f'header length exceeds file size ({header_len} > {file_size-8})'\n        header_bytes = f.read(header_len)\n        if len(header_bytes) != header_len:\n            return False, 'short read on header'\n        try:\n            json.loads(header_bytes)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return False, f'header json parse failed: {e}'\n    return True, None\n\nfor user_4813494d in roots:\n    if not user_4813494d.exists():\n        summary[str(user_4813494d)] = {'exists': False}\n        continue\n\n    data = {\n        'exists': True,\n        'asset_files': [],\n        'json_files': [],\n        'index_files': [],\n        'total_bytes': 0,\n        'by_ext': defaultdict(lambda: {'count': 0, 'bytes': 0}),\n        'errors': []\n    }\n\n    for p in user_4813494d.rglob('*'):\n        if not p.is_file():\n            continue\n        suffix = p.suffix.lower()\n        if suffix in exts:\n            sz = p.stat().st_size\n            data['asset_files'].append((p, sz))\n            data['total_bytes'] += sz\n            data['by_ext'][suffix]['count'] += 1\n            data['by_ext'][suffix]['bytes'] += sz\n        elif suffix in json_ext:\n            data['json_files'].append(p)\n            if p.name.endswith('.index.json'):\n                data['index_files'].append(p)\n\n    # JSON parse checks\n    for jp in data['json_files']:\n        try:\n            with jp.open('r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                json.load(f)\n        except Exception as e:\n            data['errors'].append((jp, f'json parse failed: {e}'))\n\n    # safetensors header checks\n    for ap, _ in data['asset_files']:\n        if ap.suffix.lower() == '.safetensors':\n            ok, err = check_safetensors_header(ap)\n            if not ok:\n                data['errors'].append((ap, f'safetensors invalid: {err}'))\n\n    # index references\n    for ip in data['index_files']:\n        try:\n            obj = json.loads(ip.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n            wm = obj.get('weight_map')\n            if isinstance(wm, dict):\n                missing = sorted({v for v in wm.values() if not (ip.parent / v).exists()})\n                for m in missing:\n                    data['errors'].append((ip, f'missing shard referenced: {m}'))\n            else:\n                data['errors'].append((ip, 'missing/invalid weight_map'))\n        except Exception as e:\n            data['errors'].append((ip, f'index validation failed: {e}'))\n\n    summary[str(user_4813494d)] = data\n    all_errors.extend([(str(p), msg) for p, msg in data['errors']])\n\n# Output compact report\nprint('=== Model Asset Integrity Report ===')\nfor user_4813494d, data in summary.items():\n    print(f'\\n[{user_4813494d}]')\n    if not data.get('exists'):\n        print('  status: MISSING')\n        continue\n    print('  status: OK' if not data['errors'] else '  status: ISSUES_FOUND')\n    print(f\"  asset_files: {len(data['asset_files'])}\")\n    print(f\"  json_files: {len(data['json_files'])} (index_json: {len(data['index_files'])})\")\n    print(f\"  total_asset_size: {human(data['total_bytes'])}\")\n    if data['by_ext']:\n        ext_line = ', '.join(\n            f\"{k}:{v['count']} ({human(v['bytes'])})\"\n            for k, v in sorted(data['by_ext'].items())\n        )\n        print(f\"  by_ext: {ext_line}\")\n\nif all_errors:\n    print(f'\\nerrors: {len(all_errors)}')\n    for p, msg in all_errors[:80]:\n        print(f'  - {p}: {msg}')\n    if len(all_errors) > 80:\n        print(f'  ... and {len(all_errors)-80} more')\nelse:\n    print('\\nerrors: 0')\n\n# Top-level inventory for /mnt/private/models\nmroot = Path('/mnt/private/models')\nif mroot.exists():\n    print('\\n=== /mnt/private/models Top-level Inventory ===')\n    rows = []\n    for child in sorted(mroot.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name.lower()):\n        if not child.is_dir():\n            continue\n        count = 0\n        total = 0\n        for p in child.rglob('*'):\n            if p.is_file() and p.suffix.lower() in exts:\n                count += 1\n                total += p.stat().st_size\n        rows.append((child.name, count, total))\n    rows.sort(key=lambda t: t[2], reverse=True)\n    for name, count, total in rows:\n        if count == 0:\n            continue\n        print(f'  - {name}: {count} files, {human(total)}')\nPY"}

{"output": "import torch\n\nMODEL_PATH = [REDACTED]\n\nprint(\"=== 加载模型组件 ===\")\n\n# 1. 加载 transformer (FP8)\nprint(\"1/5: 加载 transformer (FP8)...\")\nfrom diffusers import QwenImageTransformer2DModel\ntransformer = QwenImageTransformer2DModel.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"transformer\", torch_dtype=torch.float8_e4m3fn\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\n# 2. 加载 VAE (bf16)\nprint(\"2/5: 加载 VAE (bf16)...\")\nfrom diffusers import AutoencoderKLQwenImage\nvae = AutoencoderKLQwenImage.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"vae\", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\n# 3. 加载 text_encoder (bf16)\nprint(\"3/5: 加载 text_encoder (bf16)...\")\nfrom transformers import Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration\ntext_encoder = Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"text_encoder\", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\n# 4. 加载 tokenizer\nprint(\"4/5: 加载 tokenizer...\")\nfrom transformers import Qwen2Tokenizer\ntokenizer = Qwen2Tokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"tokenizer\")\n\n# 5. 加载 scheduler\nprint(\"5/5: 加载 scheduler...\")\nfrom diffusers import FlowMatchEulerDiscreteScheduler\nscheduler = FlowMatchEulerDiscreteScheduler.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"scheduler\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== 加载 Lightning LoRA ===\")\n# 加载 Lightning LoRA\nfrom diffusers import DiffusionPipeline\npipe = DiffusionPipeline(\n    tokenizer=tokenizer,\n    text_encoder=text_encoder,\n    vae=vae,\n    transformer=transformer,\n    scheduler=scheduler,\n    device=\"cuda\"\n)\n\n# 手动加载 LoRA\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\nprint(\"加载 LoRA weights...\")\nlora_state = load_file(f\"{MODEL_PATH}/Qwen-Image-2512-Lightning-4steps-V1.0-bf16.safetensors\")\n\n# 应用 LoRA\nprint(\"应用 LoRA...\")\npipe.transformer.load_state_dict(lora_state, strict=False)\n\nprint(\"\\n=== 推理 ===\")\nimage = pipe(\n    prompt=\"A beautiful sunset over the ocean\",\n    negative_prompt=\"blurry, low quality\",\n    num_inference_steps=4,\n    guidance_scale=1.0,\n    width=1024,\n    height=1024,\n).images[0]\n\nimage.save(\"/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/output.png\")\nprint(\"完成! 保存到 /mnt/private/workspace/wzy/output.png\")", "wall_time": "0.0512 seconds"}

{"cmd": "python - <<'PY'\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path([REDACTED])\n\nissues = []\nsummary = {}\n\n# 1) collect files\nall_files = [p for p in ROOT.rglob('*') if p.is_file()]\nsummary['file_count'] = len(all_files)\nsummary['total_bytes'] = sum(p.stat().st_size for p in all_files)\nsummary['zero_byte_files'] = [str(p.relative_to(ROOT)) for p in all_files if p.stat().st_size == 0]\n\n# 2) json parse check\njson_files = [p for p in all_files if p.suffix == '.json']\njson_ok = 0\nfor p in json_files:\n    try:\n        with p.open('r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.load(f)\n        json_ok += 1\n    except Exception as e:\n        issues.append(f'JSON parse failed: {p.relative_to(ROOT)} -> {e}')\nsummary['json_files'] = len(json_files)\nsummary['json_ok'] = json_ok\n\n# 3) index coverage check\nfor idx in [\n    ROOT / 'text_encoder' / 'model.safetensors.index.json',\n    ROOT / 'transformer' / 'diffusion_pytorch_model.safetensors.index.json',\n]:\n    try:\n        with idx.open('r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n        weight_map = data.get('weight_map', {})\n        referenced = sorted(set(weight_map.values()))\n        missing = []\n        size_sum = 0\n        for fn in referenced:\n            fp = idx.parent / fn\n            if not fp.exists():\n                missing.append(fn)\n            else:\n                size_sum += fp.stat().st_size\n        meta_total = data.get('metadata', {}).get('total_size')\n        if missing:\n            issues.append(f'Missing shards in {idx.relative_to(ROOT)}: {missing}')\n        if isinstance(meta_total, int) and meta_total != size_sum:\n            issues.append(\n                f'Metadata total_size mismatch in {idx.relative_to(ROOT)}: expected {meta_total}, got {size_sum}'\n            )\n        # check extra shards that match naming pattern but unused\n        import re\n        if 'text_encoder' in str(idx):\n            patt = re.compile(r'^model-\\d{5}-of-\\d{5}\\.safetensors$')\n        else:\n            patt = re.compile(r'^diffusion_pytorch_model-\\d{5}-of-\\d{5}\\.safetensors$')\n        existing = sorted([p.name for p in idx.parent.iterdir() if p.is_file() and patt.match(p.name)])\n        extra = sorted(set(existing) - set(referenced))\n        if extra:\n            issues.append(f'Unreferenced shard files in {idx.parent.relative_to(ROOT)}: {extra}')\n\n        summary[str(idx.relative_to(ROOT))] = {\n            'referenced_shards': len(referenced),\n            'referenced_weights': len(weight_map),\n            'size_sum': size_sum,\n            'meta_total_size': meta_total,\n        }\n    except Exception as e:\n        issues.append(f'Index check failed: {idx.relative_to(ROOT)} -> {e}')\n\n# 4) safetensors header check\nsafetensors_files = [p for p in all_files if p.suffix == '.safetensors']\nsummary['safetensors_files'] = len(safetensors_files)\n\ntry:\n    from safetensors import safe_open\nexcept Exception as e:\n    issues.append(f'safetensors import failed: {e}')\n    safe_open = None\n\nheader_ok = 0\nif safe_open is not None:\n    for p in sorted(safetensors_files):\n        rel = p.relative_to(ROOT)\n        try:\n            with safe_open(str(p), framework='pt', device='cpu') as f:\n                # force header parsing + basic metadata access\n                _ = f.keys()\n                _ = f.metadata()\n            header_ok += 1\n        except Exception as e:\n            issues.append(f'safetensors header parse failed: {rel} -> {e}')\n\nsummary['safetensors_header_ok'] = header_ok\n\nprint('=== MODEL ASSET INTEGRITY SUMMARY ===')\nprint(f\"user_4813494d: {ROOT}\")\nprint(f\"files: {summary['file_count']}\")\nprint(f\"total_bytes: {summary['total_bytes']}\")\nprint(f\"json: {summary['json_ok']}/{summary['json_files']} ok\")\nprint(f\"safetensors_header: {summary['safetensors_header_ok']}/{summary['safetensors_files']} ok\")\nprint(f\"zero_byte_files: {len(summary['zero_byte_files'])}\")\n\nfor k, v in summary.items():\n    if isinstance(v, dict):\n        print(f\"{k}: {v}\")\n\nif issues:\n    print('\\n=== ISSUES ===')\n    for i, it in enumerate(issues, 1):\n        print(f'{i}. {it}')\nelse:\n    print('\\nNo integrity issues detected in file/index/header checks.')\nPY"}

You are welcome to try the latest model at [Qwen Chat](https://chat.qwen.ai/?inputFeature=image_edit). Compared to the base Qwen-Image model released in August, Qwen-Image-2512 features the following key improvements:\n\n* **Enhanced Huamn Realism** Qwen-Image-2512 significantly reduces the “AI-generated” look and substantially enhances overall image realism, especially for human subjects.\n* **Finer Natural Detail** Qwen-Image-2512 delivers notably more detailed rendering of landscapes, animal fur, and other natural elements.\n* **Improved Text Rendering** Qwen-Image-2512 improves the accuracy and quality of textual elements, achieving better layout and more faithful multimodal (text + image) composition.\n\n## Model Performance\n\nWe conducted over 10,000 rounds of blind model evaluations on [AI Arena](https://aiarena.alibaba-inc.com/corpora/arena/leaderboard?arenaType=T2I), and the results show that Qwen-Image-2512 is currently the strongest open-source model—while remaining highly competitive even among closed-source models.\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/arena.png#center)\n\n\n## Quick Start\n\nInstall the latest version of diffusers\n```\npip install git+https://github.com/huggingface/diffusers\n```\n\nThe following contains a code snippet illustrating how to use `Qwen-Image-2512`:\n\n```python\nfrom diffusers import DiffusionPipeline\nimport torch\n\nmodel_name = \"Qwen/Qwen-Image-2512\"\n\n# Load the pipeline\nif torch.cuda.is_available():\n    torch_dtype = torch.bfloat16\n    device = \"cuda\"\nelse:\n    torch_dtype = torch.float32\n    device = \"cpu\"\n\npipe = DiffusionPipeline.from_pretrained(model_name, torch_dtype=torch_dtype).to(device)\n\n# Generate image\nprompt = '''A 20-year-old East Asian girl with delicate, charming features and large, bright brown eyes—expressive and lively, with a cheerful or subtly smiling expression. Her naturally wavy long hair is either loose or tied in twin ponytails. She has fair skin and light makeup accentuating her youthful freshness. She wears a modern, cute dress or relaxed outfit in bright, soft colors—lightweight fabric, minimalist cut. She stands indoors at an anime convention, surrounded by banners, posters, or stalls. Lighting is typical indoor illumination—no staged lighting—and the image resembles a casual iPhone snapshot: unpretentious composition, yet brimming with vivid, fresh, youthful charm.'''\n\nnegative_prompt = \"低分辨率，低画质，肢体畸形，手指畸形，画面过饱和，蜡像感，人脸无细节，过度光滑，画面具有AI感。构图混乱。文字模糊，扭曲。\"\n\n\n# Generate with different aspect ratios\naspect_ratios = {\n    \"1:1\": (1328, 1328),\n    \"16:9\": (1664, 928),\n    \"9:16\": (928, 1664),\n    \"4:3\": (1472, 1104),\n    \"3:4\": (1104, 1472),\n    \"3:2\": (1584, 1056),\n    \"2:3\": (1056, 1584),\n}\n\nwidth, height = aspect_ratios[\"16:9\"]\n\nimage = pipe(\n    prompt=prompt,\n    negative_prompt=negative_prompt,\n    width=width,\n    height=height,\n    num_inference_steps=50,\n    true_cfg_scale=4.0,\n    generator=torch.Generator(device=\"cuda\").manual_seed(42)\n).images[0]\n\nimage.save(\"example.png\")\n\n```\n\n## Showcase\n**Enhanced Huamn Realism**\n\nIn Qwen-Image-2512, human depiction has been substantially refined. Compared to the August release, Qwen-Image-2512 adds significantly richer facial details and better environmental context. For example:\n\n\n> A Chinese female college student, around 20 years old, with a very short haircut that conveys a gentle, artistic vibe. Her hair naturally falls to partially cover her cheeks, projecting a tomboyish yet charming demeanor. She has cool-toned fair skin and delicate features, with a slightly shy yet subtly confident expression—her mouth crooked in a playful, youthful smirk. She wears an off-shoulder top, revealing one shoulder, with a well-proportioned figure. The image is framed as a close-up selfie: she dominates the foreground, while the background clearly shows her dormitory—a neatly made bed with white linens on the top bunk, a tidy study desk with organized stationery, and wooden cabinets and drawers. The photo is captured on a smartphone under soft, even ambient lighting, with natural tones, high clarity, and a bright, lively atmosphere full of youthful, everyday energy.\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片1.JPG#center)\n\nFor the same prompt, Qwen-Image-2512 yields notably more lifelike facial features, and background objects—e.g., the desk, stationery, and bedding—are rendered with significantly greater clarity than in Qwen-Image.\n\n\n> A 20-year-old East Asian girl with delicate, charming features and large, bright brown eyes—expressive and lively, with a cheerful or subtly smiling expression. Her naturally wavy long hair is either loose or tied in twin ponytails. She has fair skin and light makeup accentuating her youthful freshness. She wears a modern, cute dress or relaxed outfit in bright, soft colors—lightweight fabric, minimalist cut. She stands indoors at an anime convention, surrounded by banners, posters, or stalls. Lighting is typical indoor illumination—no staged lighting—and the image resembles a casual iPhone snapshot: unpretentious composition, yet brimming with vivid, fresh, youthful charm.\n\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片2.JPG#center)\n\nHere, hair strands serve as a key differentiator: Qwen-Image’s August version tends to blur them together, losing fine detail, whereas Qwen-Image-2512 renders individual strands with precision, resulting in a more natural and realistic appearance.\n\nAnother case:\n\n> An East Asian teenage boy, aged 15–18, with soft, fluffy black short hair and refined facial contours. His large, warm brown eyes sparkle with energy. His fair skin and sunny, open smile convey an approachable, friendly demeanor—no makeup or blemishes. He wears a blue-and-white summer uniform shirt, slightly unbuttoned, made of thin breathable fabric, with black headphones hanging around his neck. His hands are in his pockets, body leaning slightly forward in a relaxed pose, as if engaged in conversation. Behind him lies a summer school playground: lush green grass and a red rubber track in the foreground, blurred school buildings in the distance, a clear blue sky with fluffy white clouds. The bright, airy lighting evokes a joyful, carefree adolescent atmosphere.\n\n\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片3.JPG#center)\n\nIn this example, Qwen-Image-2512 better adheres to semantic instructions—for instance, the prompt specifies “body leaning slightly forward,” and Qwen-Image-2512 accurately captures this posture, unlike its predecessor.\n\n\n> An elderly Chinese couple in their 70s in a clean, organized home kitchen. The woman has a kind face and a warm smile, wearing a patterned apron; the man stands behind her, also smiling, as they both gaze at a steaming pot of buns on the stove. The kitchen is bright and tidy, exuding warmth and harmony. The scene is captured with a wide-angle lens to fully show the subjects and their surroundings.\n\n\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片4.JPG#center)\n\nThis comparison starkly highlights the gap between the August and December models. The original Qwen-Image struggles to accurately render aged facial features (e.g., wrinkles), resulting in an artificial “AI look.” In contrast, Qwen-Image-2512 precisely captures age cues, dramatically boosting realism.\n\n\n\n**Finer Natural Detail**\n\nQwen-Image-2512’s enhanced detail rendering extends beyond humans—to landscapes, wildlife, and more. For instance:\n\n\n> A turquoise river winds through a lush canyon. Thick moss and dense ferns blanket the rocky walls; multiple waterfalls cascade from above, enveloped in mist. At noon, sunlight filters through the dense canopy, dappling the river surface with shimmering light. The atmosphere is humid and fresh, pulsing with primal jungle vitality. No humans, text, or artificial traces present.\n\n\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片5.JPG#center)\n\nSide-by-side, Qwen-Image-2512 exhibits superior fidelity in water flow, foliage, and waterfall mist—and renders richer gradation in greens. Another example (wave rendering):\n\n\n> At dawn, a thin mist veils the sea. An ancient stone lighthouse stands at the cliff’s edge, its beacon faintly visible through the fog. Black rocks are pounded by waves, sending up bursts of white spray. The sky glows in soft blue-purple hues under cool, hazy light—evoking solitude and solemn grandeur.\n\n\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片6.JPG#center)\n\nFur detail is another highlight—here, a golden retriever portrait:\n\n\n> An ultra-realistic close-up of a golden retriever outdoors under soft daylight. Hair is exquisitely detailed: strands distinct, color transitioning naturally from warm gold to light cream, light glinting delicately at the tips; a gentle breeze adds subtle volume. Undercoat is soft and dense; guard hairs are long and well-defined, with visible layering. Eyes are moist, expressive; nose is slightly damp with fine specular highlights. Background is softly blurred to emphasize the dog’s tangible texture and vivid expression.\n\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片7.JPG#center)\n\n\n\n\nSimilarly, texture quality improves in depictions of rugged wildlife—for example, a male argali sheep:\n\n\n> A male argali stands atop a barren, rocky mountainside. Its coarse, dense grey-brown coat covers a powerful, muscular body. Most striking are its massive, thick, outward-spiraling horns—a symbol of wild strength. Its gaze is alert and sharp. The background reveals steep alpine terrain: jagged peaks, sparse low vegetation, and abundant sunlight—conveying the harsh yet majestic wilderness and the animal’s resilient vitality.\n\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片8.JPG#center)\n\n**Improved Text Rendering**\n\nQwen-Image-2512 further elevates text rendering—already a strength of the original—by improving accuracy, layout, and multimodal integration.\n\nFor instance, this prompt requests a complete PPT slide illustrating Qwen-Image’s development roadmap (generation and editing tracks):\n\n> 这是一张现代风格的科技感幻灯片，整体采用深蓝色渐变背景。标题是“Qwen-Image发展历程”。下方一条水平延伸的发光时间轴，轴线中间写着“生图路线”。由左侧淡蓝色渐变为右侧深紫色，并以精致的箭头收尾。时间轴上每个节点通过虚线连接至下方醒目的蓝色圆角矩形日期标签，标签内为清晰白色字体，从左向右依次写着：“2025年5月6日 Qwen-Image 项目启动”“2025年8月4日  Qwen-Image 开源发布”“2025年12月31日 Qwen-Image-2512 开源发布” （周围光晕显著）在下方一条水平延伸的发光时间轴，轴线中间写着“编辑路线”。由左侧淡蓝色渐变为右侧深紫色，并以精致的箭头收尾。时间轴上每个节点通过虚线连接至下方醒目的蓝色圆角矩形日期标签，标签内为清晰白色字体，从左向右依次写着：“2025年8月18日 Qwen-Image-Edit 开源发布”“2025年9月22日 Qwen-Image-Edit-2509 开源发布”“2025年12月19日 Qwen-Image-Layered 开源发布”“2025年12月23日 Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 开源发布”\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片9.JPG#center)\n\nWe can even generate a before-and-after comparison slide to highlight the leap from “AI-blurry” to “photorealistic”:\n\n\n> 这是一张现代风格的科技感幻灯片，整体采用深蓝色渐变背景。顶部中央为白色无衬线粗体大字标题“Qwen-Image-2512重磅发布”。画面主体为横向对比图，视觉焦点集中于中间的升级对比区域。左侧为面部光滑没有任何细节的女性人像，质感差；右侧为高度写实的年轻女性肖像，皮肤呈现真实毛孔纹理与细微光影变化，发丝根根分明，眼眸透亮，表情自然，整体质感接近写实摄影。两图像之间以一个绿色流线型箭头链接。造型科技感十足，中部标注“2512质感升级”，使用白色加粗字体，居中显示。箭头两侧有微弱光晕效果，增强动态感。在图像下方，以白色文字呈现三行说明：“● 更真实的人物质感。大幅度降低了生成图片的AI感，提升了图像真实性 ● 更细腻的自然纹理。大幅度提升了生成图片的纹理细节。风景图，动物毛发刻画更细腻。● 更复杂的文字渲染。大幅提升了文字渲染的质量。图文混合渲染更准确，排版更好”\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片10.JPG#center)\n\nA more complex infographic example:\n\n\n\n> 这是一幅专业级工业技术信息图表，整体采用深蓝色科技感背景，光线均匀柔和，营造出冷静、精准的现代工业氛围。画面分为左右两大板块，布局清晰，视觉层次分明。左侧板块标题为“实际发生的现象”，以浅蓝色圆角矩形框突出显示，内部排列三个深蓝色按钮式条目，第一个条目展示一堆棕色粉末状原料上滴落水滴的图标，文字为“团聚/结块”，后面配有绿色对钩；第二个条目为一个装有蓝色液体并冒出气泡的锥形瓶，文字为“产生气泡/缺陷”，后面配有绿色对钩；第三个条目为两个生锈的齿轮，文字为“设备腐蚀/催化剂失活”，后面配有绿色对钩。右侧板块标题为“【不会】发生的现象”，使用米黄色圆角矩形框呈现，内部四个条目均置于深灰色背景方框中。图标分别为：一组精密啮合的金属齿轮，文字为“反应效率【显著提高】”，上方覆盖醒目的红色叉号；一捆整齐排列的金属管材，文字为“成品内部【绝对无气泡/孔隙】”，上方覆盖醒目的红色叉号；一条坚固的金属链条正在承受拉力，文字为“材料强度与耐久性【得到增强】”，上方覆盖醒目的红色叉号；一堆腐蚀的扳手，文字为“加工过程【零腐蚀/零副反应风险】”，上方覆盖醒目的红色叉号。底部中央有一行小字注释：“注：水分的存在通常会导致负面或干扰性的结果，而非理想或增强的状态”，字体为白色，清晰可读。整体风格现代简约，配色对比强烈，图形符号准确传达技术逻辑，适合用于工业培训或科普演示场景。\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片11.JPG#center)\n\nOr even a full educational poster:\n\n\n> 这是一幅由十二个分格组成的3×4网格布局的写实摄影作品，整体呈现“健康的一天”主题，画面风格简洁清晰，每一分格独立成景又统一于生活节奏的叙事脉络。第一行分别是“06:00 晨跑唤醒身体”：面部特写，一位女性身穿灰色运动套装，背景是初升的朝阳与葱郁绿树；“06:30 动态拉伸激活关节”：女性身着瑜伽服在阳台做晨间拉伸，身体舒展，背景为淡粉色天空与远山轮廓；“07:30 均衡营养早餐”：桌上摆放全麦面包、牛油果和一杯橙汁，女性微笑着准备用餐；“08:00 补水润燥”：透明玻璃水杯中浮有柠檬片，女性手持水杯轻啜，阳光从左侧斜照入室，杯壁水珠滑落；第二行分别是：“09:00 专注高效工作”：女性专注敲击键盘，屏幕显示简洁界面，身旁放有一杯咖啡与一盆绿植；“12:00 静心阅读时光”：女性坐在书桌前翻阅纸质书籍，台灯散发暖光，书页泛黄，旁放半杯红茶；“12:30 午后轻松漫步”：女性在林荫道上漫步，脸部特写；“15:00 茶香伴午后”：女性端着骨瓷茶杯站在窗边，窗外是城市街景与飘动云朵，茶香袅袅；第三行分别是：“18:00 运动释放压力”：健身房内，女性正在练习瑜伽；“19:00 美味晚餐”：女性在开放式厨房中切菜，砧板上有番茄与青椒，锅中热气升腾，灯光温暖；“21:00 冥想助眠”：女性盘腿坐在柔软地毯上冥想，双手轻放膝上，闭目宁静；“21:30 进入睡眠”：女性躺在床上休息。整体采用自然光线为主，色调以暖白与米灰为基调，光影层次分明，画面充满温馨的生活气息与规律的节奏感。\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片12.JPG#center)\n\n\nThese are the core enhancements in this update. We hope you enjoy using Qwen-Image-2512!\n\n\n\n## Citation\nIf Qwen-Image-2512 proves helpful in your research, we’d greatly appreciate your citation 📝 :)\n\n\n\n```BibTeX\n@misc{wu2025qwenimagetechnicalreport,\n      title={Qwen-Image Technical Report}, \n      author={Chenfei Wu and Jiahao Li and Jingren Zhou and Junyang Lin and Kaiyuan Gao and Kun Yan and Sheng-ming Yin and Shuai Bai and Xiao Xu and Yilei Chen and Yuxiang Chen and Zecheng Tang and Zekai Zhang and Zhengyi Wang and An Yang and Bowen Yu and Chen Cheng and Dayiheng Liu and Deqing Li and Hang Zhang and Hao Meng and Hu Wei and Jingyuan Ni and Kai Chen and Kuan Cao and Liang Peng and Lin Qu and Minggang Wu and Peng Wang and Shuting Yu and Tingkun Wen and Wensen Feng and Xiaoxiao Xu and Yi Wang and Yichang Zhang and Yongqiang Zhu and Yujia Wu and Yuxuan Cai and Zenan Liu},\n      year={2025},\n      eprint={2508.02324},\n      archivePrefix={arXiv},\n      primaryClass={cs.CV},\n      url={https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02324}, \n}\n```", "wall_time": "0.0512 seconds"}

{"output": "import torch\n\nMODEL_PATH = [REDACTED]\n\nprint(\"=== 加载模型组件 ===\")\n\n# 1. 加载 transformer (FP8)\nprint(\"1/5: 加载 transformer (FP8)...\")\nfrom diffusers import QwenImageTransformer2DModel\ntransformer = QwenImageTransformer2DModel.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"transformer\", torch_dtype=torch.float8_e4m3fn\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\n# 2. 加载 VAE (bf16)\nprint(\"2/5: 加载 VAE (bf16)...\")\nfrom diffusers import AutoencoderKLQwenImage\nvae = AutoencoderKLQwenImage.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"vae\", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\n# 3. 加载 text_encoder (bf16)\nprint(\"3/5: 加载 text_encoder (bf16)...\")\nfrom transformers import Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration\ntext_encoder = Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"text_encoder\", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\n# 4. 加载 tokenizer\nprint(\"4/5: 加载 tokenizer...\")\nfrom transformers import Qwen2Tokenizer\ntokenizer = Qwen2Tokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"tokenizer\")\n\n# 5. 加载 scheduler\nprint(\"5/5: 加载 scheduler...\")\nfrom diffusers import FlowMatchEulerDiscreteScheduler\nscheduler = FlowMatchEulerDiscreteScheduler.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"scheduler\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== 加载 Lightning LoRA ===\")\n# 加载 Lightning LoRA - 使用原路径\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\n\n# 检查 LoRA 文件\nLORA_PATH = [REDACTED]\nprint(\"加载 LoRA weights...\")\nlora_state = load_file(f\"{LORA_PATH}/Qwen-Image-2512-Lightning-4steps-V1.0-bf16.safetensors\")\n\n# 构建 pipeline\nfrom diffusers import DiffusionPipeline\npipe = DiffusionPipeline(\n    tokenizer=tokenizer,\n    text_encoder=text_encoder,\n    vae=vae,\n    transformer=transformer,\n    scheduler=scheduler,\n    device=\"cuda\"\n)\n\n# 应用 LoRA\nprint(\"应用 LoRA...\")\n# LoRA weights 需要加到 transformer 上\nfor key in lora_state:\n    if key in pipe.transformer.state_dict():\n        pipe.transformer.state_dict()[key] = lora_state[key]\n\nprint(\"\\n=== 推理 ===\")\nimage = pipe(\n    prompt=\"A beautiful sunset over the ocean\",\n    negative_prompt=\"blurry, low quality\",\n    num_inference_steps=4,\n    guidance_scale=1.0,\n    width=1024,\n    height=1024,\n).images[0]\n\nimage.save(\"/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/output.png\")\nprint(\"完成! 保存到 /mnt/private/workspace/wzy/output.png\")", "wall_time": "0.0505 seconds"}

{"output": "import torch\nimport gc\n\nMODEL_PATH = [REDACTED]\n\nprint(\"=== Step 1: 加载 text_encoder (CPU) ===\")\nfrom transformers import Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration, Qwen2Tokenizer\n\ntext_encoder = Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"text_encoder\", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16\n)\ntokenizer = Qwen2Tokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"tokenizer\")\n\nprint(\"Text encoder loaded on CPU\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 2: 编码 prompt ===\")\nprompt = \"A beautiful sunset over the ocean\"\nnegative_prompt = \"blurry, low quality\"\n\n# 编码\ntext_inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\"pt\", padding=True)\nnegative_inputs = tokenizer(negative_prompt, return_tensors=\"pt\", padding=True)\n\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    prompt_embeds = text_encoder(**text_inputs, return_dict=True).logits\n    negative_prompt_embeds = text_encoder(**negative_inputs, return_dict=True).logits\n\n# 释放 text_encoder 和 tokenizer\ndel text_encoder, tokenizer, text_inputs, negative_inputs\ngc.collect()\n\nprint(f\"Prompt embeds shape: {prompt_embeds.shape}\")\nprint(f\"Negative embeds shape: {negative_prompt_embeds.shape}\")\n\n# 保存到 CPU\nprompt_embeds_cpu = prompt_embeds.cpu()\nnegative_prompt_embeds_cpu = negative_prompt_embeds.cpu()\ndel prompt_embeds, negative_prompt_embeds\ngc.collect()\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 3: 加载 transformer (FP8) ===\")\nfrom diffusers import QwenImageTransformer2DModel\ntorch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\ntransformer = QwenImageTransformer2DModel.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"transformer\", torch_dtype=torch.float8_e4m3fn\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\nprint(\"Transformer loaded on GPU (FP8)\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 4: 加载 VAE ===\")\nfrom diffusers import AutoencoderKLQwenImage\nvae = AutoencoderKLQwenImage.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"vae\", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\nprint(\"VAE loaded on GPU\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 5: 加载 scheduler ===\")\nfrom diffusers import FlowMatchEulerDiscreteScheduler\nscheduler = FlowMatchEulerDiscreteScheduler.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"scheduler\")\n\nprint(\"Scheduler loaded\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 6: 加载 Lightning LoRA ===\")\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\nfrom diffusers import DiffusionPipeline\n\nLORA_PATH = [REDACTED]\nprint(\"加载 LoRA weights...\")\nlora_state = load_file(f\"{LORA_PATH}/Qwen-Image-2512-Lightning-4steps-V1.0-bf16.safetensors\")\n\n# 应用 LoRA\nprint(\"应用 LoRA...\")\ntransformer.load_state_dict(lora_state, strict=False)\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Step 7: 推理 ===\")\n\n# 移动 embeds 到 GPU\nprompt_embeds = prompt_embeds_cpu.to(\"cuda\")\nnegative_prompt_embeds = negative_prompt_embeds_cpu.to(\"cuda\")\n\n# 简化推理 - 直接使用 pipe\npipe = DiffusionPipeline(\n    tokenizer=tokenizer,\n    text_encoder=None,\n    vae=vae,\n    transformer=transformer,\n    scheduler=scheduler,\n    device=\"cuda\"\n)\n\n# 使用预计算的 embeds\nfrom diffusers import FlowMatchEulerDiscreteScheduler\n\n# 设置 timestep\nscheduler.set_timesteps(4)\n\nlatents = torch.randn(\n    (1, 16, 128, 128),\n    device=\"cuda\",\n    dtype=torch.float8_e4m3fn\n)\n\nfor i, t in enumerate(scheduler.timesteps):\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        # 简化 - 只用 transformer 预测\n        model_pred = transformer(\n            sample=latents,\n            timestep=t,\n            encoder_hidden_states=prompt_embeds,\n        ).sample\n\n    latents = scheduler.step(model_pred, t, latents).prev_sample\n\nprint(\"解码...\")\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    image = vae.decode(latents).sample\n\nimage = (image / 2 + 0.5).clamp(0, 1)\nimage = image.cpu().float()\n\nfrom PIL import Image\nimage = Image.fromarray((image[0].permute(1, 2, 0).numpy() * 255).astype(\"uint8\"))\n\nimage.save(\"/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/output.png\")\nprint(\"完成!\")", "wall_time": "0.0516 seconds"}

{"patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: run_real_fp8_chain.py\n+import gc\n+import os\n+import time\n+import traceback\n+from pathlib import Path\n+\n+import torch\n+\n+\n+MODEL_PATH = Path([REDACTED])\n+OUTPUT_IMAGE = Path(\"/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/output_fp8_dit_lora_cfg1_steps4.png\")\n+FP8_DIT_FILE = MODEL_PATH / \"qwen_image_2512_fp8_e4m3fn.safetensors\"\n+LORA_FILE = [REDACTED]\n+\n+PROMPT = \"A cinematic photo of a silver robot walking in light rain on a neon city street at night.\"\n+NEGATIVE_PROMPT = \"blurry, low quality, distorted, bad anatomy, watermark\"\n+SEED = 42\n+\n+\n+def log(msg: str) -> None:\n+    ts = time.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n+    print(f\"[{ts}] {msg}\", flush=True)\n+\n+\n+def cuda_mem() -> str:\n+    if not torch.cuda.is_available():\n+        return \"cuda_unavailable\"\n+    alloc = torch.cuda.memory_allocated() / (1024**3)\n+    reserved = torch.cuda.memory_reserved() / (1024**3)\n+    return f\"alloc={alloc:.2f}GB reserved={reserved:.2f}GB\"\n+\n+\n+def clear_cuda() -> None:\n+    gc.collect()\n+    if torch.cuda.is_available():\n+        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n+\n+\n+def load_fp8_transformer(model_path: Path, fp8_path: Path):\n+    from diffusers import QwenImageTransformer2DModel\n+\n+    try:\n+        log(\"Loading FP8 DiT via from_single_file...\")\n+        transformer = QwenImageTransformer2DModel.from_single_file(\n+            str(fp8_path),\n+            config=str(model_path / \"transformer\" / \"config.json\"),\n+            torch_dtype=torch.float8_e4m3fn,\n+        )\n+        return transformer, \"from_single_file\"\n+    except Exception as e:\n+        log(f\"from_single_file failed: {e}\")\n+        log(\"Fallback: instantiate from config and load safetensors manually...\")\n+        from safetensors.torch import load_file\n+\n+        config = QwenImageTransformer2DModel.load_config(str(model_path), subfolder=\"transformer\")\n+        transformer = QwenImageTransformer2DModel.from_config(config)\n+        state = load_file(str(fp8_path), device=\"cpu\")\n+        prefix = \"model.diffusion_model.\"\n+        remapped = {}\n+        for k, v in state.items():\n+            if k.startswith(prefix):\n+                remapped[k[len(prefix) :]] = v\n+            else:\n+                remapped[k] = v\n+        missing, unexpected = transformer.load_state_dict(remapped, strict=False)\n+        log(f\"FP8 DiT load_state_dict missing={len(missing)} unexpected={len(unexpected)}\")\n+        transformer = transformer.to(dtype=torch.float8_e4m3fn)\n+        return transformer, \"manual_state_dict\"\n+\n+\n+def main() -> int:\n+    os.environ.setdefault(\"PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF\", \"expandable_segments:True\")\n+\n+    if not torch.cuda.is_available():\n+        raise RuntimeError(\"CUDA is not available. Cannot run real GPU inference.\")\n+\n+    log(f\"Torch: {torch.__version__}, CUDA: {torch.version.cuda}, GPU: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}\")\n+    log(f\"Model path: {MODEL_PATH}\")\n+\n+    # Phase 1: FP8 text_encoder test with transformers.\n+    text_test_ok = False\n+    text_test_note = \"\"\n+    try:\n+        from transformers import Qwen2Tokenizer, Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration\n+\n+        log(\"Phase 1: loading tokenizer...\")\n+        tokenizer = Qwen2Tokenizer.from_pretrained(str(MODEL_PATH), subfolder=\"tokenizer\", local_files_only=True)\n+\n+        log(\"Phase 1: trying to load text_encoder in FP8 with transformers...\")\n+        text_encoder = Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration.from_pretrained(\n+            str(MODEL_PATH),\n+            subfolder=\"text_encoder\",\n+            torch_dtype=torch.float8_e4m3fn,\n+            low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n+            local_files_only=True,\n+        ).eval().to(\"cuda\")\n+\n+        actual_dtype = next(text_encoder.parameters()).dtype\n+        log(f\"text_encoder loaded, first param dtype={actual_dtype}, {cuda_mem()}\")\n+\n+        inputs = tokenizer(\"Please answer only OK\", return_tensors=\"pt\").to(\"cuda\")\n+        with torch.inference_mode():\n+            out = text_encoder.generate(**inputs, do_sample=False, max_new_tokens=8)\n+        decoded = tokenizer.decode(out[0], skip_special_tokens=True)\n+        text_test_ok = True\n+        text_test_note = decoded\n+        log(f\"Phase 1 inference output: {decoded}\")\n+    except Exception as e:\n+        text_test_note = f\"{type(e).__name__}: {e}\"\n+        log(f\"Phase 1 failed: {text_test_note}\")\n+        log(traceback.format_exc())\n+    finally:\n+        for name in (\"text_encoder\", \"tokenizer\", \"inputs\", \"out\"):\n+            if name in locals():\n+                del locals()[name]\n+        clear_cuda()\n+        log(f\"Phase 1 cleanup done, {cuda_mem()}\")\n+\n+    # Phase 2: FP8 DiT + LoRA, cfg=1 steps=4, generate image.\n+    log(\"Phase 2: loading FP8 DiT transformer...\")\n+    transformer, load_mode = load_fp8_transformer(MODEL_PATH, FP8_DIT_FILE)\n+    log(f\"FP8 DiT ready by {load_mode}, first param dtype={next(transformer.parameters()).dtype}\")\n+\n+    from diffusers import DiffusionPipeline\n+\n+    log(\"Phase 2: building pipeline with custom FP8 transformer...\")\n+    pipe = DiffusionPipeline.from_pretrained(\n+        str(MODEL_PATH),\n+        transformer=transformer,\n+        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n+        local_files_only=True,\n+    )\n+\n+    log(\"Phase 2: loading Lightning LoRA...\")\n+    pipe.load_lora_weights(str(MODEL_PATH), weight_name=LORA_FILE)\n+\n+    log(\"Phase 2: enabling CPU offload for 24G VRAM safety...\")\n+    pipe.enable_model_cpu_offload()\n+\n+    gen = torch.Generator(device=\"cuda\").manual_seed(SEED)\n+    log(\"Phase 2: running image inference (cfg=1, steps=4)...\")\n+    t0 = time.time()\n+    with torch.inference_mode():\n+        image = pipe(\n+            prompt=PROMPT,\n+            negative_prompt=NEGATIVE_PROMPT,\n+            num_inference_steps=4,\n+            true_cfg_scale=1.0,\n+            width=1024,\n+            height=1024,\n+            generator=gen,\n+        ).images[0]\n+    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n+    image.save(OUTPUT_IMAGE)\n+    log(f\"Image saved: {OUTPUT_IMAGE} (elapsed={elapsed:.2f}s)\")\n+\n+    log(\"=== FINAL SUMMARY ===\")\n+    log(f\"phase1_fp8_text_encoder_ok={text_test_ok}\")\n+    log(f\"phase1_note={text_test_note}\")\n+    log(f\"phase2_output={OUTPUT_IMAGE}\")\n+    return 0\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    raise SystemExit(main())\n*** End Patch\n"}

{"patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: run_real_fp8_chain.py\n@@\n-    log(\"Phase 2: running image inference (cfg=1, steps=4)...\")\n-    t0 = time.time()\n-    with torch.inference_mode():\n-        image = pipe(\n-            prompt=PROMPT,\n-            negative_prompt=NEGATIVE_PROMPT,\n-            num_inference_steps=4,\n-            true_cfg_scale=1.0,\n-            width=1024,\n-            height=1024,\n-            generator=gen,\n-        ).images[0]\n+    def run_pipe_once(seed: int):\n+        local_gen = torch.Generator(device=\"cuda\").manual_seed(seed)\n+        with torch.inference_mode():\n+            return pipe(\n+                prompt=PROMPT,\n+                negative_prompt=NEGATIVE_PROMPT,\n+                num_inference_steps=4,\n+                true_cfg_scale=1.0,\n+                width=1024,\n+                height=1024,\n+                generator=local_gen,\n+            ).images[0]\n+\n+    log(\"Phase 2: running image inference (cfg=1, steps=4)...\")\n+    t0 = time.time()\n+    try:\n+        image = run_pipe_once(SEED)\n+    except RuntimeError as e:\n+        err = str(e)\n+        if \"same dtype\" in err and \"Float8_e4m3fn\" in err:\n+            log(\"FP8 runtime dtype mismatch; casting transformer to BF16 and retrying inference...\")\n+            pipe.transformer.to(dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n+            clear_cuda()\n+            image = run_pipe_once(SEED)\n+        else:\n+            raise\n*** End Patch\n"}

this adapter keeps memory in FP8 inside DiT\n+    # while returning bf16 outputs to the scheduler.\n+    fp8_dtype = torch.float8_e4m3fn\n+    _orig_forward = pipe.transformer.forward\n+\n+    def _fp8_forward(\n+        hidden_states,\n+        encoder_hidden_states=None,\n+        encoder_hidden_states_mask=None,\n+        timestep=None,\n+        img_shapes=None,\n+        txt_seq_lens=None,\n+        guidance=None,\n+        attention_kwargs=None,\n+        controlnet_block_samples=None,\n+        return_dict=True,\n+    ):\n+        hidden_states = hidden_states.to(fp8_dtype)\n+        if encoder_hidden_states is not None and torch.is_floating_point(encoder_hidden_states):\n+            encoder_hidden_states = encoder_hidden_states.to(fp8_dtype)\n+        if guidance is not None and torch.is_floating_point(guidance):\n+            guidance = guidance.to(fp8_dtype)\n+\n+        out = _orig_forward(\n+            hidden_states=hidden_states,\n+            encoder_hidden_states=encoder_hidden_states,\n+            encoder_hidden_states_mask=encoder_hidden_states_mask,\n+            timestep=timestep,\n+            img_shapes=img_shapes,\n+            txt_seq_lens=txt_seq_lens,\n+            guidance=guidance,\n+            attention_kwargs=attention_kwargs,\n+            controlnet_block_samples=controlnet_block_samples,\n+            return_dict=return_dict,\n+        )\n+        if return_dict and hasattr(out, \"sample\") and torch.is_tensor(out.sample):\n+            out.sample = out.sample.to(torch.bfloat16)\n+            return out\n+        if torch.is_tensor(out):\n+            return out.to(torch.bfloat16)\n+        return out\n+\n+    pipe.transformer.forward = _fp8_forward\n+\n     log(\"Phase 2: enabling CPU offload for 24G VRAM safety...\")\n     pipe.enable_model_cpu_offload()\n*** End Patch\n"}

> AGENT

根因确认了：当前 `torch 2.6.0+cu124` 在 4090 上不支持 `Float8_e4m3fn` 的 `addmm`（线性层核心算子），所以 FP8 DiT 无法实跑。我要升级到更新版 PyTorch（带更完整 FP8 内核）再复测。

{"output": "drop_idx = self.prompt_template_encode_start_idx\n        txt = [template.format(e) for e in prompt]\n        txt_tokens = self.tokenizer(\n            txt, max_length=self.tokenizer_max_length + drop_idx, padding=True, truncation=True, return_tensors=\"pt\"\n        ).to(device)\n        encoder_hidden_states = self.text_encoder(\n            input_ids=txt_tokens.input_ids,\n            attention_mask=txt_tokens.attention_mask,\n            output_hidden_states=True,\n        )\n        hidden_states = encoder_hidden_states.hidden_states[-1]\n        split_hidden_states = self._extract_masked_hidden(hidden_states, txt_tokens.attention_mask)\n        split_hidden_states = [e[drop_idx:] for e in split_hidden_states]\n        attn_mask_list = [torch.ones(e.size(0), dtype=torch.long, device=e.device) for e in split_hidden_states]\n        max_seq_len = max([e.size(0) for e in split_hidden_states])\n        prompt_embeds = torch.stack(\n            [torch.cat([u, u.new_zeros(max_seq_len - u.size(0), u.size(1))]) for u in split_hidden_states]\n        )\n        encoder_attention_mask = torch.stack(\n            [torch.cat([u, u.new_zeros(max_seq_len - u.size(0))]) for u in attn_mask_list]\n        )\n\n        prompt_embeds = prompt_embeds.to(dtype=dtype, device=device)\n\n        return prompt_embeds, encoder_attention_mask\n\n    def encode_prompt(\n        self,\n        prompt: Union[str, List[str]],\n        device: Optional[torch.device] = None,\n        num_images_per_prompt: int = 1,\n        prompt_embeds: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n        prompt_embeds_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n        max_sequence_length: int = 1024,\n    ):\n        r\"\"\"\n\n        Args:\n            prompt (`str` or `List[str]`, *optional*):\n                prompt to be encoded\n            device: (`torch.device`):\n                torch device\n            num_images_per_prompt (`int`):\n                number of images that should be generated per prompt\n            prompt_embeds (`torch.Tensor`, *optional*):\n                Pre-generated text embeddings. Can be used to easily tweak text inputs, *e.g.* prompt weighting. If not\n                provided, text embeddings will be generated from `prompt` input argument.\n        \"\"\"\n        device = device or self._execution_device\n\n        prompt = [prompt] if isinstance(prompt, str) else prompt\n        batch_size = len(prompt) if prompt_embeds is None else prompt_embeds.shape[0]\n\n        if prompt_embeds is None:\n            prompt_embeds, prompt_embeds_mask = self._get_qwen_prompt_embeds(prompt, device)\n\n        prompt_embeds = prompt_embeds[:, :max_sequence_length]\n        prompt_embeds_mask = prompt_embeds_mask[:, :max_sequence_length]\n\n        _, seq_len, _ = prompt_embeds.shape\n        prompt_embeds = prompt_embeds.repeat(1, num_images_per_prompt, 1)\n        prompt_embeds = prompt_embeds.view(batch_size * num_images_per_prompt, seq_len, -1)\n        prompt_embeds_mask = prompt_embeds_mask.repeat(1, num_images_per_prompt, 1)\n        prompt_embeds_mask = prompt_embeds_mask.view(batch_size * num_images_per_prompt, seq_len)\n\n        return prompt_embeds, prompt_embeds_mask\n\n    def check_inputs(\n        self,\n        prompt,\n        height,\n        width,\n        negative_prompt=None,\n        prompt_embeds=None,\n        negative_prompt_embeds=None,\n        prompt_embeds_mask=None,\n        negative_prompt_embeds_mask=None,\n        callback_on_step_end_tensor_inputs=None,\n        max_sequence_length=None,\n    ):\n        if height % (self.vae_scale_factor * 2) != 0 or width % (self.vae_scale_factor * 2) != 0:\n            logger.warning(\n                f\"`height` and `width` have to be divisible by {self.vae_scale_factor * 2} but are {height} and {width}. Dimensions will be resized accordingly\"\n            )\n\n        if callback_on_step_end_tensor_inputs is not None and not all(\n            k in self._callback_tensor_inputs for k in callback_on_step_end_tensor_inputs\n        ):\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"`callback_on_step_end_tensor_inputs` has to be in {self._callback_tensor_inputs}, but found {[k for k in callback_on_step_end_tensor_inputs if k not in self._callback_tensor_inputs]}\"\n            )\n\n        if prompt is not None and prompt_embeds is not None:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"Cannot forward both `prompt`: {prompt} and `prompt_embeds`: {prompt_embeds}. Please make sure to\"\n                \" only forward one of the two.\"\n            )\n        elif prompt is None and prompt_embeds is None:\n            raise ValueError(\n                \"Provide either `prompt` or `prompt_embeds`. Cannot leave both `prompt` and `prompt_embeds` undefined.\"\n            )\n        elif prompt is not None and (not isinstance(prompt, str) and not isinstance(prompt, list)):\n            raise ValueError(f\"`prompt` has to be of type `str` or `list` but is {type(prompt)}\")\n\n        if negative_prompt is not None and negative_prompt_embeds is not None:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"Cannot forward both `negative_prompt`: {negative_prompt} and `negative_prompt_embeds`:\"\n                f\" {negative_prompt_embeds}. src=\"https://qianwen-res.oss-accelerate-overseas.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/image2512big.png#center\" width=\"1600\"/>\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:25:We are excited to introduce Qwen-Image-2512, the December update of Qwen-Image’s text-to-image foundational model. You are welcome to try the latest model at [Qwen Chat](https://chat.qwen.ai/?inputFeature=image_edit). Compared to the base Qwen-Image model released in August, Qwen-Image-2512 features the following key improvements:\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:27:* **Enhanced Huamn Realism** Qwen-Image-2512 significantly reduces the “AI-generated” look and substantially enhances overall image realism, especially for human subjects.\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:28:* **Finer Natural Detail** Qwen-Image-2512 delivers notably more detailed rendering of landscapes, animal fur, and other natural elements.\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:29:* **Improved Text Rendering** Qwen-Image-2512 improves the accuracy and quality of textual elements, achieving better layout and more faithful multimodal (text + image) composition.\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:33:We conducted over 10,000 rounds of blind model evaluations on [AI Arena](https://aiarena.alibaba-inc.com/corpora/arena/leaderboard?arenaType=T2I), and the results show that Qwen-Image-2512 is currently the strongest open-source model—while remaining highly competitive even among closed-source models.\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:35:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/arena.png#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:45:The following contains a code snippet illustrating how to use `Qwen-Image-2512`:\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:51:model_name = \"Qwen/Qwen-Image-2512\"\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:88:    true_cfg_scale=4.0,\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:99:In Qwen-Image-2512, human depiction has been substantially refined. Compared to the August release, Qwen-Image-2512 adds significantly richer facial details and better environmental context. For example:\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:104:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片1.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:106:For the same prompt, Qwen-Image-2512 yields notably more lifelike facial features, and background objects—e.g., the desk, stationery, and bedding—are rendered with significantly greater clarity than in Qwen-Image.\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:112:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片2.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:114:Here, hair strands serve as a key differentiator: Qwen-Image’s August version tends to blur them together, losing fine detail, whereas Qwen-Image-2512 renders individual strands with precision, resulting in a more natural and realistic appearance.\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:122:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片3.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:124:In this example, Qwen-Image-2512 better adheres to semantic instructions—for instance, the prompt specifies “body leaning slightly forward,” and Qwen-Image-2512 accurately captures this posture, unlike its predecessor.\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:131:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片4.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:133:This comparison starkly highlights the gap between the August and December models. The original Qwen-Image struggles to accurately render aged facial features (e.g., wrinkles), resulting in an artificial “AI look.” In contrast, Qwen-Image-2512 precisely captures age cues, dramatically boosting realism.\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:139:Qwen-Image-2512’s enhanced detail rendering extends beyond humans—to landscapes, wildlife, and more. For instance:\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:146:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片5.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:148:Side-by-side, Qwen-Image-2512 exhibits superior fidelity in water flow, foliage, and waterfall mist—and renders richer gradation in greens. Another example (wave rendering):\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:155:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片6.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:163:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片7.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:174:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片8.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:178:Qwen-Image-2512 further elevates text rendering—already a strength of the original—by improving accuracy, layout, and multimodal integration.\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:180:For instance, this prompt requests a complete PPT slide illustrating Qwen-Image’s development roadmap (generation and editing tracks):\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:182:> 这是一张现代风格的科技感幻灯片，整体采用深蓝色渐变背景。标题是“Qwen-Image发展历程”。下方一条水平延伸的发光时间轴，轴线中间写着“生图路线”。由左侧淡蓝色渐变为右侧深紫色，并以精致的箭头收尾。时间轴上每个节点通过虚线连接至下方醒目的蓝色圆角矩形日期标签，标签内为清晰白色字体，从左向右依次写着：“2025年5月6日 Qwen-Image 项目启动”“2025年8月4日  Qwen-Image 开源发布”“2025年12月31日 Qwen-Image-2512 开源发布” （周围光晕显著）在下方一条水平延伸的发光时间轴，轴线中间写着“编辑路线”。由左侧淡蓝色渐变为右侧深紫色，并以精致的箭头收尾。时间轴上每个节点通过虚线连接至下方醒目的蓝色圆角矩形日期标签，标签内为清晰白色字体，从左向右依次写着：“2025年8月18日 Qwen-Image-Edit 开源发布”“2025年9月22日 Qwen-Image-Edit-2509 开源发布”“2025年12月19日 Qwen-Image-Layered 开源发布”“2025年12月23日 Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 开源发布”\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:184:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片9.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:189:> 这是一张现代风格的科技感幻灯片，整体采用深蓝色渐变背景。顶部中央为白色无衬线粗体大字标题“Qwen-Image-2512重磅发布”。画面主体为横向对比图，视觉焦点集中于中间的升级对比区域。左侧为面部光滑没有任何细节的女性人像，质感差；右侧为高度写实的年轻女性肖像，皮肤呈现真实毛孔纹理与细微光影变化，发丝根根分明，眼眸透亮，表情自然，整体质感接近写实摄影。两图像之间以一个绿色流线型箭头链接。造型科技感十足，中部标注“2512质感升级”，使用白色加粗字体，居中显示。箭头两侧有微弱光晕效果，增强动态感。在图像下方，以白色文字呈现三行说明：“● 更真实的人物质感。大幅度降低了生成图片的AI感，提升了图像真实性 ● 更细腻的自然纹理。大幅度提升了生成图片的纹理细节。风景图，动物毛发刻画更细腻。● 更复杂的文字渲染。大幅提升了文字渲染的质量。图文混合渲染更准确，排版更好”\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:191:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片10.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:199:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片11.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:206:![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片12.JPG#center)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:209:These are the core enhancements in this update. We hope you enjoy using Qwen-Image-2512!\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:214:If Qwen-Image-2512 proves helpful in your research, we’d greatly appreciate your citation 📝 :)\n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/README.md:220:      title={Qwen-Image Technical Report}, \n/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/Qwen-Image-2512/tokenizer/vocab.json:130389:  \"Ġlightning\": 32438,", "wall_time": "0.0508 seconds"}

{"output": "import torch\nfrom diffusers import DiffusionPipeline\n\nMODEL_PATH = [REDACTED]\n\n# 使用本地模型路径加载 pipeline (bf16)\nprint(\"加载 pipeline (bf16)...\")\npipe = DiffusionPipeline.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH,\n    torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\n# 加载 Lightning LoRA (使用 bf16)\nprint(\"加载 Lightning LoRA...\")\npipe.load_lora_weights(\n    MODEL_PATH,\n    weight_name=[REDACTED]\n)\n\n# 手动转换 transformer 为 FP8\nprint(\"转换 transformer 为 FP8...\")\nfor name, param in pipe.transformer.named_parameters():\n    if param.dtype == torch.bfloat16:\n        param.data = param.data.to(torch.float8_e4m3fn)\n\n# 推理: CFG=1, steps=4\nprint(\"推理中...\")\nimage = pipe(\n    prompt=\"A beautiful sunset over the ocean\",\n    negative_prompt=\"blurry, low quality\",\n    num_inference_steps=4,\n    guidance_scale=1.0,\n    width=1024,\n    height=1024,\n).images[0]\n\nimage.save(\"/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/output.png\")\nprint(\"完成!\")", "wall_time": "0.0508 seconds"}

{"output": "import torch\n\nMODEL_PATH = [REDACTED]\n\nprint(\"=== 加载模型组件 ===\")\n\n# 1. 加载 transformer (FP8)\nprint(\"1/5: 加载 transformer (FP8)...\")\nfrom diffusers import QwenImageTransformer2DModel\ntransformer = QwenImageTransformer2DModel.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"transformer\", torch_dtype=torch.float8_e4m3fn\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\n# 2. 加载 VAE (bf16)\nprint(\"2/5: 加载 VAE (bf16)...\")\nfrom diffusers import AutoencoderKLQwenImage\nvae = AutoencoderKLQwenImage.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"vae\", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\n# 3. 加载 text_encoder (bf16)\nprint(\"3/5: 加载 text_encoder (bf16)...\")\nfrom transformers import Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration\ntext_encoder = Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"text_encoder\", torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16\n).to(\"cuda\")\n\n# 4. 加载 tokenizer\nprint(\"4/5: 加载 tokenizer...\")\nfrom transformers import Qwen2Tokenizer\ntokenizer = Qwen2Tokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"tokenizer\")\n\n# 5. 加载 scheduler\nprint(\"5/5: 加载 scheduler...\")\nfrom diffusers import FlowMatchEulerDiscreteScheduler\nscheduler = FlowMatchEulerDiscreteScheduler.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, subfolder=\"scheduler\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== 加载 Lightning LoRA ===\")\n# 加载 Lightning LoRA\nfrom diffusers import DiffusionPipeline\npipe = DiffusionPipeline(\n    tokenizer=tokenizer,\n    text_encoder=text_encoder,\n    vae=vae,\n    transformer=transformer,\n    scheduler=scheduler,\n    device=\"cuda\"\n)\n\n# 手动加载 LoRA\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\nprint(\"加载 LoRA weights...\")\nlora_state = load_file(f\"{MODEL_PATH}/Qwen-Image-2512-Lightning-4steps-V1.0-bf16.safetensors\")\n\n# 应用 LoRA\nprint(\"应用 LoRA...\")\npipe.transformer.load_state_dict(lora_state, strict=False)\n\nprint(\"\\n=== 推理 ===\")\nimage = pipe(\n    prompt=\"A beautiful sunset over the ocean\",\n    negative_prompt=\"blurry, low quality\",\n    num_inference_steps=4,\n    guidance_scale=1.0,\n    width=1024,\n    height=1024,\n).images[0]\n\nimage.save(\"/mnt/private/workspace/wzy/output.png\")\nprint(\"完成! 保存到 /mnt/private/workspace/wzy/output.png\")", "wall_time": "0.0507 seconds"}

{"output": "Install the latest version of diffusers\n```\npip install git+https://github.com/huggingface/diffusers\n```\n\nThe following contains a code snippet illustrating how to use `Qwen-Image-2512`:\n\n```python\nfrom diffusers import DiffusionPipeline\nimport torch\n\nmodel_name = \"Qwen/Qwen-Image-2512\"\n\n# Load the pipeline\nif torch.cuda.is_available():\n    torch_dtype = torch.bfloat16\n    device = \"cuda\"\nelse:\n    torch_dtype = torch.float32\n    device = \"cpu\"\n\npipe = DiffusionPipeline.from_pretrained(model_name, torch_dtype=torch_dtype).to(device)\n\n# Generate image\nprompt = '''A 20-year-old East Asian girl with delicate, charming features and large, bright brown eyes—expressive and lively, with a cheerful or subtly smiling expression. Her naturally wavy long hair is either loose or tied in twin ponytails. She has fair skin and light makeup accentuating her youthful freshness. She wears a modern, cute dress or relaxed outfit in bright, soft colors—lightweight fabric, minimalist cut. She stands indoors at an anime convention, surrounded by banners, posters, or stalls. Lighting is typical indoor illumination—no staged lighting—and the image resembles a casual iPhone snapshot: unpretentious composition, yet brimming with vivid, fresh, youthful charm.'''\n\nnegative_prompt = \"低分辨率，低画质，肢体畸形，手指畸形，画面过饱和，蜡像感，人脸无细节，过度光滑，画面具有AI感。构图混乱。文字模糊，扭曲。\"\n\n\n# Generate with different aspect ratios\naspect_ratios = {\n    \"1:1\": (1328, 1328),\n    \"16:9\": (1664, 928),\n    \"9:16\": (928, 1664),\n    \"4:3\": (1472, 1104),\n    \"3:4\": (1104, 1472),\n    \"3:2\": (1584, 1056),\n    \"2:3\": (1056, 1584),\n}\n\nwidth, height = aspect_ratios[\"16:9\"]\n\nimage = pipe(\n    prompt=prompt,\n    negative_prompt=negative_prompt,\n    width=width,\n    height=height,\n    num_inference_steps=50,\n    true_cfg_scale=4.0,\n    generator=torch.Generator(device=\"cuda\").manual_seed(42)\n).images[0]\n\nimage.save(\"example.png\")\n\n```\n\n## Showcase\n**Enhanced Huamn Realism**\n\nIn Qwen-Image-2512, human depiction has been substantially refined. Compared to the August release, Qwen-Image-2512 adds significantly richer facial details and better environmental context. For example:\n\n\n> A Chinese female college student, around 20 years old, with a very short haircut that conveys a gentle, artistic vibe. Her hair naturally falls to partially cover her cheeks, projecting a tomboyish yet charming demeanor. She has cool-toned fair skin and delicate features, with a slightly shy yet subtly confident expression—her mouth crooked in a playful, youthful smirk. She wears an off-shoulder top, revealing one shoulder, with a well-proportioned figure. The image is framed as a close-up selfie: she dominates the foreground, while the background clearly shows her dormitory—a neatly made bed with white linens on the top bunk, a tidy study desk with organized stationery, and wooden cabinets and drawers. The photo is captured on a smartphone under soft, even ambient lighting, with natural tones, high clarity, and a bright, lively atmosphere full of youthful, everyday energy.\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片1.JPG#center)\n\nFor the same prompt, Qwen-Image-2512 yields notably more lifelike facial features, and background objects—e.g., the desk, stationery, and bedding—are rendered with significantly greater clarity than in Qwen-Image.\n\n\n> A 20-year-old East Asian girl with delicate, charming features and large, bright brown eyes—expressive and lively, with a cheerful or subtly smiling expression. Her naturally wavy long hair is either loose or tied in twin ponytails. She has fair skin and light makeup accentuating her youthful freshness. She wears a modern, cute dress or relaxed outfit in bright, soft colors—lightweight fabric, minimalist cut. She stands indoors at an anime convention, surrounded by banners, posters, or stalls. Lighting is typical indoor illumination—no staged lighting—and the image resembles a casual iPhone snapshot: unpretentious composition, yet brimming with vivid, fresh, youthful charm.\n\n\n![](https://qianwen-res.oss-cn-beijing.aliyuncs.com/Qwen-Image/image2512/幻灯片2.JPG#center)\n\nHere, hair strands serve as a key differentiator: Qwen-Image’s August version tends to blur them together, losing fine detail, whereas Qwen-Image-2512 renders individual strands with precision, resulting in a more natural and realistic appearance.", "wall_time": "0.0520 seconds"}
